## Finalized Report / Current Recommendation

Treat [UID:0000HF] as the sole physical source unit for the complete byte-alpha surface family and reconstruct it as `NexusTK/render/AlphaMaskSurface.cpp` plus `AlphaMaskSurface.h`. Fresh RTTI, vtable-write, function-order, range, caller, and generated-output evidence proves that the concrete dynamic class implemented by the entire `0x00462120-0x004632b1` family is `IntAlphaSurface`. The historical `AlphaMaskSurface` name is a project-era semantic label, not the recovered C++ class name.

The callback-applied accepted source shape therefore:

- define `IntAlphaSurface`, `AlphaSurfaceBufferView`, and `AlphaMaskBlendMode` in `AlphaMaskSurface.h`, with every emitted declaration protected by an effective guard;
- emit thirteen ordinary source methods in exact address/source order through exact by-memory children;
- retain the raw default constructor as ordinary source despite zero static references;
- fold the provisional `IntAlphaSurface.cpp` split into [UID:0000HF];
- make the aggregate, semantic alias, layout duplicate, vtable pages, EH cleanup, scalar-delete mechanics, and zero-reference `ret 0x20` stub non-emitting evidence where the compiler recreates the binary artifact;
- correct every consumer declaration from `AlphaMaskSurface` to `IntAlphaSurface`;
- preserve the exact encoded-stream contract: a null `encodedMaskBytes` pointer is a no-op, zero tokens terminate rows, the high bit selects filled runs, and each run length is the unsigned token masked with `0x7f`;
- replace all six dated empty emitters with formal source or an exact non-emitting disposition;
- keep the supervisor-owned Gate 2B handoff deterministic: every I05 function `set_type` stage is followed by exact typed-state readback; generic `arg_*` rows may adopt the signature's names/types automatically, while only the six listed still-mismatched rows receive a separate collision-checked public stack-row rename. I05.13 additionally applies one public occupied-stack `set_type` because its literal frame type remains `char` after successful function typing; the name-only rename receives no type-change credit. Every pre-I05 local, saved-register, return-address, and unrelated argument row remains exact. Formal source and decompiler signatures retain human unqualified UDT names, while literal `stack_frame` contracts accept only the exact endpoint-specific name/tag/const spelling listed for that stage.

Gate 2B runtime authority and persistence are supervisor-owned external procedures. The supervisor must establish fresh runtime, backup, save, readback, promotion, and rollback safety and fail closed if any prerequisite is unavailable. The removed legacy operational package is retained only as inert, non-authoritative history in [0000HF-AlphaMaskSurface-file-source-quality-removed.md](./0000HF-AlphaMaskSurface-file-source-quality-removed.md).

The corrected formal CPP/H below resolves the previously defective header composition and encoded-stream source shape rather than deferring them. No blocker is deferred as future research. Remaining confidence limits are the unrecoverable exact original spellings of several private methods/fields, the zero-reference liveness of the default constructor and `nullsub_24`, and the absence of a rebuilt-binary comparison. Those limitations do not justify keeping decompiler names or empty emitters.

## Supporting Research

The research pass reconciled the then-current target/support by-* pages, validator output, seven earlier child-focused B reports, and dated live read-only IDA MCP evidence. Earlier reports remain evidence leads; none substitutes for the compilation-unit inventory required here.

The source-reconstruction research below is independent of persistence mechanics. Removed legacy operational persistence text is linked above only as inert provenance and supplies no action or credit.

The decisive correction is that all apparently split `AlphaMaskSurface` and `IntAlphaSurface` behavior belongs to one concrete class:

- the only class RTTI string is `.?AVIntAlphaSurface@@`;
- its complete-object locator, hierarchy descriptor, base descriptor, and type descriptor form a one-class hierarchy with no recovered base;
- the same vtable at `0x006112ec` is written by both constructors, view construction, EH cleanup, and the destructor;
- the vtable slots are the scalar deleting destructor and owned-pixel release helper;
- no `AlphaMaskSurface` RTTI string, second class hierarchy, second vtable, or independent constructor family exists.

The other major correction is that `DrawEncodedAlphaFrame` is a logical `void` method. All nine callers ignore EAX, including the three raw caller bodies whose exact boundaries were recovered. Its machine return is stale register residue, not a source return contract. The body also preserves the observed stream semantics: `encodedMaskBytes == NULL` returns without destination work; otherwise `IntersectRects` populates the clipped bounds but its return value does not introduce an unobserved early exit, byte zero terminates each encoded row, bit `0x80` classifies a filled run, and `token & 0x7f` supplies the run length.

The external supervisor procedure must establish fresh runtime, backup, save, persistence readback, promotion, and rollback safety and fail closed when any prerequisite is unavailable; no session-role, filesystem, save, promotion, restoration, or cleanup transaction is specified here.

## Inference Research Guidance Check

- Wave2/Wave3 labels and workflow references were ignored as stale.
- Binary/runtime behavior takes priority over style consistency.
- Human source shape was inferred after exact behavior, ranges, callers, fields, and compiler artifacts were established.
- No finalized recommendation preserves `sub_`, `off_`, raw-address, or decompiler-shaped source names.
- Negative evidence was used to reject false split/ownership/return claims, not to avoid a best source-facing decision.
- The final class name is directly recovered from RTTI; method and field spellings without symbols use the most probable consistent project style.
- This is first-party NexusTK render source. No third-party embed/import directive applies.

## Heuristic / Inference Reanalysis And Validation

| Question | Alternatives tested | Positive/negative evidence | Best current conclusion |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Concrete class name | `AlphaMaskSurface`; `IntAlphaSurface`; two related classes | Only `IntAlphaSurface` RTTI exists; one hierarchy and one vtable; all constructor/destructor/view writes use it; no AlphaMask RTTI/vtable | Concrete class is `IntAlphaSurface`; AlphaMaskSurface is historical semantic labeling |
| Physical file split | AlphaMaskSurface.cpp plus IntAlphaSurface.cpp; Surface.cpp fold; one AlphaMaskSurface module | Contiguous family; no second class family; target path already accepted; separate generated file contains only two methods from the same class | One `AlphaMaskSurface.cpp/.h`; fold provisional IntAlphaSurface.cpp |
| Raw default constructor | padding/dead bytes; compiler artifact; ordinary retained constructor | Exact source-shaped body, vtable install, field initialization, RectBounds call, `this` return; zero refs/pointers | Ordinary source default constructor retained under no-reference evidence |
| `0x00462300` return | returns output pointer; void accessor | EAX is output-pointer residue; both callers consume caller storage and ignore EAX | `void GetBufferInfo(AlphaSurfaceBufferView*) const` |
| `0x00462320` return | int/bool; output pointer; void | Sole caller uses caller storage and ignores EAX; success/failure represented by output pixels | `void GetClippedBufferInfo(...)` |
| `DrawEncodedAlphaFrame` return | pointer; status; void | All nine callers ignore EAX; residue is not semantically stable | `void` |
| `DrawEncodedAlphaFrame` stream decode | signed magnitude; high-bit tagged runs; defensive clipping return | Null stream is an observed no-op; zero terminates a row; bit 7 selects filled data; low seven bits are length; the `IntersectRects` result is not an authored early-return condition | Unsigned token decode with `token & 0x7f`, high-bit filled-run test, zero row terminators, and no invented intersection-result branch |
| Aggregate emission | one monolithic body; comment-only aggregate; exact children | Thirteen independent source methods plus compiler ranges/padding | Aggregate is non-emitting audit map; exact children own source |
| Vtable emission | handwritten array; class-generated compiler data | RTTI-backed ordinary virtual class; vtable has compiler-owned COL prefix and scalar-delete wrapper | No handwritten vtable CPP |
| Null stub | source callback; virtual method; linker artifact | Exact `ret 0x20`, zero xrefs and pointer hits, outside vtable slots | Compiler/linker-retained no-code exact page |
| Gradient naming | decompiler names; generic fill; radial/elliptic | Parameter use and equations distinguish circular radius from anisotropic ellipse | `FillRadialGradient` and `FillEllipticGradient` |

Score-improvement attempts resolved the prior blockers rather than restating them:

- raw constructor liveness was searched through function, code/data/immediate, VA/RVA/raw-offset, and caller evidence;
- every raw DrawEncoded caller boundary was recovered and its post-call EAX use checked;
- the DrawEncoded body was rechecked for null-stream, row-terminator, high-bit classification, low-seven-bit run length, and intersection-result control-flow semantics;
- class identity and split were resolved through RTTI/vtable/write topology;
- missing exact child ranges were enumerated with hashes and padding;
- source declarations/includes were checked against current generated headers;
- all six empty emitters received exact source or no-code dispositions.

## Evidence Standards Used

- Exact address ranges are half-open.
- Body hashes use SHA256 over current canonical bytes.
- Caller inventories distinguish modeled and raw callers.
- Static liveness uses code xrefs, data xrefs, immediate/VA/RVA/raw-offset pointer searches, and vtable/data stores.
- Return contracts require caller consumption, not decompiler residue.
- Class identity uses RTTI, vtable topology, constructor/destructor stores, layout, and call behavior together.
- Source placement uses physical adjacency plus coherent ownership and excludes compiler/linker reordering as sole proof.
- Formal C++ preserves observed unchecked behavior; it does not add defensive guards absent from the binary.
- Encoded-byte semantics are derived from the raw unsigned token: zero terminates a row, `0x80` classifies the filled-run path, and `0x7f` supplies length; signed-magnitude decoding is rejected.

## Positive Evidence Summary

- Unique `IntAlphaSurface` RTTI provides a direct original class name.
- Constructor, view, EH, and destructor vtable writes unify the complete family.
- Exact layout is stable across every method: bounds at `+0x04`, pixels `+0x14`, width `+0x18`, height `+0x1c`, stride `+0x20`, owns flag `+0x24`.
- Thirteen ordinary methods have coherent object use, source order, and dependencies.
- Raw constructor bytes are a complete ordinary constructor body.
- All caller-facing return questions are resolved from caller behavior.
- All nine DrawEncoded callers and three raw caller bodies are closed.
- DrawEncoded stream control is exact: null data is a no-op, zero terminates each row, the high bit selects a filled run, and the low seven bits supply its length; no signed-magnitude or intersection-result early-return source is introduced.
- ByteSpan helpers, EPFTileContext, RectBounds, Point, and image-library consumers have existing project documentation.
- The exact physical vtable and RTTI relationships are bounded and hashable.

## Documentation Evidence And IDA Status

The pre-callback documentation captured many local behaviors, but the callback corrected these superseded claims:

- `AlphaMaskSurface` and `IntAlphaSurface` are not separate source classes.
- `IntAlphaSurface.cpp` is not an independent physical unit.
- `DrawEncodedAlphaFrame` is not blocked from formal source by EAX residue.
- `DrawEncodedAlphaFrame` must not use signed-magnitude run lengths or an invented `IntersectRects` early return, and must preserve the observed null-stream no-op.
- `BuildClippedAlphaSurfaceView` is not a standalone by-item emitter.
- `AlphaMaskSurfaceLayout` is not a second source declaration.
- vtable pages should not emit handwritten arrays.
- dated command 25040 omissions are historical pre-callback state.

The dated read-only MCP snapshots retained mostly raw names/types: `sub_462170`, `sub_462260`, `sub_4622D0`, `sub_462320`, `sub_4623C0`, `sub_462520`, `sub_462680`, `sub_462870`, `sub_462E10`, `sub_463270`, and `off_6112EC`. Their two semantic names/comments at 0x462300 and 0x462f20 said AlphaMaskSurface and are recommendations for correction, not permanent authority. The fresh dated snapshot supersedes the earlier broad snapshot only for the three operational persisted/analysis type fields at `0x4622d0`, `0x462320`, and `0x462520`; immediate transaction-local Gate 2B preflight remains the only mutation authority.

## Ranked Ownership Analysis

1. **IntAlphaSurface class in AlphaMaskSurface.cpp/.h - very high.** Unique RTTI, one vtable, contiguous family, all vtable stores, exact common layout, and target source path agree.
2. **Separate IntAlphaSurface.cpp - rejected.** It is a documentation-era split created from destructor/vtable naming; no second constructor, RTTI, vtable, range family, or physical separation supports it.
3. **AlphaMaskSurface as a second class - rejected.** No RTTI or second dynamic identity exists.
4. **Surface.cpp fold - low.** Shared consumers/dependencies do not override the coherent dedicated family and accepted target path.
5. **Image-library ownership - rejected.** Light/Item/Static/Human/Map routines are callers; they do not own the surface implementation.

## Source Placement

Accepted physical placement:

- `NexusTK/render/AlphaMaskSurface.h`: separately guarded `AlphaSurfaceBufferView`, followed by owning-header-guarded `AlphaMaskBlendMode`, `IntAlphaSurface`, and all nested method declarations.
- `NexusTK/render/AlphaMaskSurface.cpp`: includes and all thirteen exact source definitions in ascending address order.

The source filename preserves a historical subsystem name even though the concrete class is `IntAlphaSurface`; this is more probable than inventing a separate physical file contradicted by the binary grouping. The callback retained [UID:0000K8] as historical documentation of the rejected split rather than an emitting source file.

Class-level source belongs on [UID:00006K]. Exact method source belongs on exact by-memory pages nested through [UID:00006K]. The file page owns the physical output route. Consumer pages retain their own source ownership and receive only corrected declarations/includes/type spellings.

## First-Draft C++ Recommendation

These are formal callback insertion payloads, not prose examples. They preserve C++03-era style and observed behavior. The exact method pages own their own CPP/H; [UID:00006K] owns the file preamble and class shell; [UID:0001TQ] owns the POD declaration.

### UID00006K Formal H

```cpp
#ifndef NEXUSTK_RENDER_ALPHAMASKSURFACE_H
#define NEXUSTK_RENDER_ALPHAMASKSURFACE_H

#include "../ui/core/RectBounds.h"

struct EPFTileContext;

enum AlphaMaskBlendMode
{
    kAlphaMaskBlendCopy = 0,
    kAlphaMaskBlendAdd = 1,
    kAlphaMaskBlendSubtract = 2
};

class IntAlphaSurface
{
public:
    virtual ~IntAlphaSurface();
    virtual void ReleaseOwnedPixels();

    [[CHILDREN]]

private:
    RectBounds m_bounds;
    unsigned char *m_pixels;
    int m_width;
    int m_height;
    int m_stride;
    bool m_ownsBuffer;
    unsigned char m_padding[3];
};

#endif
```

The UID0001TQ H fragment must precede UID00006K H so `AlphaSurfaceBufferView` is complete before child declarations reference it. Its narrow guard is required because the POD is an independently emitted pre-class fragment; the UID00006K owning-header guard separately encloses the blend enum, class shell, and every nested method declaration. Thus every declaration in the composed header is guarded without moving the POD behind a use site. UID00006K CPP must be the owning preamble, and all method children must nest at its CPP/H `[[CHILDREN]]` markers.

### UID0000YK Formal CPP

```cpp
IntAlphaSurface::IntAlphaSurface()
    : m_pixels(NULL),
      m_width(0),
      m_height(0),
      m_stride(0),
      m_ownsBuffer(true)
{
    InitRectBounds(&m_bounds, 0, 0, 0, 0);
}
```

### UID0000YK Formal H

    IntAlphaSurface();

### New 0x00462170 Constructor Formal CPP

```cpp
IntAlphaSurface::IntAlphaSurface(int width, int height)
    : m_pixels(NULL),
      m_width(0),
      m_height(0),
      m_stride(0),
      m_ownsBuffer(true)
{
    InitRectBounds(&m_bounds, 0, 0, 0, 0);

    if (width > 0 && height > 0) {
        ReleaseOwnedPixels();
        m_width = width;
        m_height = height;
        m_stride = width;
        m_pixels = static_cast<unsigned char *>(malloc(width * height));
        m_ownsBuffer = true;
        InitRectBounds(&m_bounds, 0, 0, width, height);
    }
}
```

### New 0x00462170 Constructor Formal H

```cpp
    IntAlphaSurface(int width, int height);
```

### UID0004FR Formal CPP

```cpp
void IntAlphaSurface::ReleaseOwnedPixels()
{
    if (m_ownsBuffer && m_pixels != NULL) {
        free(m_pixels);
        m_pixels = NULL;
    }
}
```

### New 0x00462320 Clipped View Formal CPP

```cpp
void IntAlphaSurface::GetClippedBufferInfo(AlphaSurfaceBufferView *outView,
                                            const RectBounds *clipBounds) const
{
    RectBounds surfaceBounds;
    RectBounds clippedBounds;
    InitRectBounds(&surfaceBounds, 0, 0, m_width, m_height);

    if (m_pixels != NULL &&
        IntersectRects(&surfaceBounds, clipBounds, &clippedBounds)) {
        outView->pixels = m_pixels
                        + clippedBounds.left
                        + clippedBounds.top * m_stride;
        outView->width = clippedBounds.right - clippedBounds.left;
        outView->height = clippedBounds.bottom - clippedBounds.top;
        outView->stride = m_stride;
    } else {
        outView->pixels = NULL;
    }

    outView->ownsBuffer = false;
}
```

IntAlphaSurface *IntAlphaSurface::InitSurfaceView(
    IntAlphaSurface *view,
    const RectBounds *clipBounds) const
{
    view = new (view) IntAlphaSurface();

    if (clipBounds == NULL) {
        view->m_pixels = m_pixels;
        view->m_width = m_width;
        view->m_height = m_height;
        view->m_stride = m_stride;
    } else if (m_pixels != NULL) {
        RectBounds surfaceBounds;
        RectBounds clippedBounds;
        InitRectBounds(&surfaceBounds, 0, 0, m_width, m_height);

        if (IntersectRects(&surfaceBounds, clipBounds, &clippedBounds)) {
            view->m_pixels = m_pixels
                           + clippedBounds.left
                           + clippedBounds.top * m_stride;
            view->m_width = clippedBounds.right - clippedBounds.left;
            view->m_height = clippedBounds.bottom - clippedBounds.top;
            view->m_stride = m_stride;
        } else {
            view->m_pixels = NULL;
        }
    } else {
        view->m_pixels = NULL;
    }

    view->m_ownsBuffer = false;
    return view;
}

### New 0x00462520 Rect Fill Formal CPP

```cpp
void IntAlphaSurface::ApplyRectAlpha(const RectBounds *bounds,
                                     unsigned char alpha,
                                     AlphaMaskBlendMode blendMode)
{
    if (m_pixels == NULL) {
        return;
    }

    RectBounds clippedBounds;
    if (bounds == NULL) {
        clippedBounds = m_bounds;
    } else if (!IntersectRects(&m_bounds, bounds, &clippedBounds)) {
        return;
    }

    const int width = clippedBounds.right - clippedBounds.left;
    for (int y = clippedBounds.top; y < clippedBounds.bottom; ++y) {
        unsigned char *pixel = m_pixels + y * m_stride + clippedBounds.left;

        if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendCopy) {
            memset(pixel, alpha, width);
        } else if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendAdd) {
            for (int x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
                pixel[x] = static_cast<unsigned char>(pixel[x] + alpha);
            }
        } else if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendSubtract) {
            for (int x = 0; x < width; ++x) {
                pixel[x] = static_cast<unsigned char>(pixel[x] - alpha);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### New 0x00462680 Radial Gradient Formal CPP

```cpp
void IntAlphaSurface::FillRadialGradient(int centerY,
                                         int centerX,
                                         int radius,
                                         unsigned char centerAlpha,
                                         unsigned char edgeAlpha,
                                         float exponent,
                                         AlphaMaskBlendMode blendMode)
{
    if (m_pixels == NULL || radius <= 0 || exponent <= 0.0f) {
        return;
    }

    RectBounds requestedBounds;
    RectBounds clippedBounds;
    InitRectBounds(&requestedBounds,
                   centerX - radius,
                   centerY - radius,
                   centerX + radius + 1,
                   centerY + radius + 1);
    if (!IntersectRects(&m_bounds, &requestedBounds, &clippedBounds)) {
        return;
    }

    const float alphaScale =
        (edgeAlpha - centerAlpha) / pow(static_cast<float>(radius), exponent);

    for (int y = clippedBounds.top; y < clippedBounds.bottom; ++y) {
        const float dy = static_cast<float>(y - centerY);
        unsigned char *pixel = m_pixels + y * m_stride + clippedBounds.left;

        for (int x = clippedBounds.left; x < clippedBounds.right; ++x, ++pixel) {
            const float dx = static_cast<float>(x - centerX);
            const float distance = static_cast<float>(sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));
            if (distance <= radius) {
                const unsigned char alpha = static_cast<unsigned char>(
                    pow(distance, exponent) * alphaScale + centerAlpha + 0.5f);

                if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendCopy) {
                    *pixel = alpha;
                } else if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendAdd) {
                    *pixel = static_cast<unsigned char>(*pixel + alpha);
                } else if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendSubtract) {
                    *pixel = static_cast<unsigned char>(*pixel - alpha);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### New 0x00462870 Elliptic Gradient Formal CPP

```cpp
void IntAlphaSurface::FillEllipticGradient(int centerY,
                                           int centerX,
                                           int radiusY,
                                           int radiusX,
                                           int innerRadius,
                                           unsigned char centerAlpha,
                                           unsigned char edgeAlpha,
                                           float exponent,
                                           AlphaMaskBlendMode blendMode)
{
    if (m_pixels == NULL ||
        innerRadius < 0 ||
        radiusY <= 0 ||
        radiusX <= 0 ||
        exponent <= 0.0f) {
        return;
    }

    RectBounds requestedBounds;
    RectBounds clippedBounds;
    InitRectBounds(&requestedBounds,
                   centerX - radiusX,
                   centerY - radiusY,
                   centerX + radiusX + 1,
                   centerY + radiusY + 1);
    if (!IntersectRects(&m_bounds, &requestedBounds, &clippedBounds)) {
        return;
    }

    const int outerRadius = radiusX > radiusY ? radiusX : radiusY;
    float alphaScale = 0.0f;
    if (innerRadius < outerRadius) {
        alphaScale = (edgeAlpha - centerAlpha) /
                     pow(static_cast<float>(outerRadius - innerRadius), exponent);
    }

    const float xScale = radiusX < radiusY
                       ? static_cast<float>(radiusY) / radiusX
                       : 1.0f;
    const float yScale = radiusY < radiusX
                       ? static_cast<float>(radiusX) / radiusY
                       : 1.0f;

    for (int y = clippedBounds.top; y < clippedBounds.bottom; ++y) {
        const float dy = static_cast<float>(y - centerY) * yScale;
        unsigned char *pixel = m_pixels + y * m_stride + clippedBounds.left;

        for (int x = clippedBounds.left; x < clippedBounds.right; ++x, ++pixel) {
            const float dx = static_cast<float>(x - centerX) * xScale;
            const float distance = static_cast<float>(sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy));
            if (distance <= outerRadius) {
                unsigned char alpha = centerAlpha;
                if (distance > innerRadius) {
                    alpha = static_cast<unsigned char>(
                        pow(distance - innerRadius, exponent) * alphaScale
                        + centerAlpha + 0.5f);
                }

                if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendCopy) {
                    *pixel = alpha;
                } else if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendAdd) {
                    *pixel = static_cast<unsigned char>(*pixel + alpha);
                } else if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendSubtract) {
                    *pixel = static_cast<unsigned char>(*pixel - alpha);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### UID0000YN Formal CPP

```cpp
void IntAlphaSurface::DrawFrameWithBlendMode(
    const RectBounds *destinationRect,
    const IntAlphaSurface *sourceFrame,
    const RectBounds *sourceRect,
    AlphaMaskBlendMode blendMode)
{
    if (m_pixels == NULL ||
        destinationRect == NULL ||
        sourceFrame == NULL ||
        sourceRect == NULL) {
        return;
    }

    RectBounds clippedDestination;
    if (!IntersectRects(&m_bounds, destinationRect, &clippedDestination)) {
        return;
    }

    const int rowCount = clippedDestination.bottom - clippedDestination.top;
    const int width = clippedDestination.right - clippedDestination.left;
    const int sourceX =
        clippedDestination.left + sourceRect->left - destinationRect->left;
    const int sourceY =
        clippedDestination.top + sourceRect->top - destinationRect->top;

    const unsigned char *source =
        sourceFrame->m_pixels + sourceX + sourceY * sourceFrame->m_stride;
    unsigned char *destination =
        m_pixels + clippedDestination.left + clippedDestination.top * m_stride;

    if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendCopy) {
        ByteSpanCopyRows(source, sourceFrame->m_stride,
                         destination, m_stride, width, rowCount);
    } else if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendAdd) {
        ByteSpanAddRows(source, sourceFrame->m_stride,
                        destination, m_stride, width, rowCount);
    } else if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendSubtract) {
        ByteSpanSubtractRows(source, sourceFrame->m_stride,
                             destination, m_stride, width, rowCount);
    }
}
```

### UID0000YO Formal CPP

```cpp
void IntAlphaSurface::DrawEncodedAlphaFrame(
    const RectBounds *destinationRect,
    const EPFTileContext *sourceContext,
    const RectBounds *sourceOrigin,
    int alphaEndY,
    unsigned char alphaEnd,
    int alphaStartY,
    unsigned char alphaStart,
    AlphaMaskBlendMode blendMode)
{
    if (sourceContext->encodedMaskBytes == NULL) {
        return;
    }

    RectBounds clippedDestination;
    IntersectRects(&m_bounds, destinationRect, &clippedDestination);

    const unsigned char *run =
        reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char *>(sourceContext->encodedMaskBytes);
    int rowsToSkip =
        sourceOrigin->top - destinationRect->top
        + clippedDestination.top - sourceContext->bounds.top;

    while (rowsToSkip-- > 0) {
        while (*run++ != 0) {
        }
    }

    const int width =
        clippedDestination.right - clippedDestination.left;
    const int sourceX =
        clippedDestination.left + sourceOrigin->left - destinationRect->left;
    const float alphaStep =
        static_cast<float>(alphaEnd - alphaStart) /
        static_cast<float>(alphaEndY - alphaStartY);

    for (int y = clippedDestination.top;
         y < clippedDestination.bottom;
         ++y) {
        const unsigned char alpha = static_cast<unsigned char>(
            (y - alphaStartY) * alphaStep + alphaStart + 0.5f);
        unsigned char *destination =
            m_pixels + y * m_stride + clippedDestination.left;
        int x = sourceContext->bounds.left - sourceX;

        unsigned char token;
        while ((token = *run++) != 0) {
            const int count = static_cast<int>(token & 0x7f);
            if ((token & 0x80) != 0) {
                int first = x;
                int last = x + count;
                if (first < 0) {
                    first = 0;
                }
                if (last > width) {
                    last = width;
                }

                for (int column = first; column < last; ++column) {
                    if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendCopy) {
                        destination[column] = alpha;
                    } else if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendAdd) {
                        destination[column] = static_cast<unsigned char>(
                            destination[column] + alpha);
                    } else if (blendMode == kAlphaMaskBlendSubtract) {
                        destination[column] = static_cast<unsigned char>(
                            destination[column] - alpha);
                    }
                }
            }
            x += count;
        }
    }
}
```
